# STRAT earnings news and SEC evidence preservation index

This extends recovery PR #1573. It preserves a reviewable index and permitted derived summaries; it does not redistribute vendor response bodies, price paths, article text, raw OHLC, secrets or private runtime manifests. Individual charts and full cases remain in the owner-only evidence Page and its existing files. File digests verify identity; an index is not a claim that the original bytes are publicly backed up or client-download verified.

Owner-only evidence Page: https://chatgpt.com/space/page_1f24ac618ab88191ba7ad80291938186

## Durable artifacts and verification limits

- Original market-results archive: 328 members; 18,239,044 bytes; SHA-256 `db97f9d12fbc208cf50008f9c1be6b872af10e2473fb710788c574ce965df8f0`. Existing private delivery and multipart-attempt receipts, where present, are indexed below; original outputs are unchanged. This index entry alone does not certify complete original bytes off-runtime.
- Latest database repair/source/export proof: 92 members; 143,113 bytes; SHA-256 `db9dd3a50d77efdeb55a37ab6b51332ce52b6318f00988cb465e8928e9a230e8`. Owner-only Page file `library-file:fde1_bGliZmlsZV9Pdno3NUlBcDdHWW05QXdpNjlmTzlB_FileDrive_10f58b7ea0a8819199c1005a411e446c`. Local members verified; service size/hash/access confirmed; ZIP member/client download readback not established.
- Original nine-event news scatter: owner-provided native Library ID `libfile_814acc60547c81919122f2a8ebfe6486`; parent reattached it. This is not a chart for the seven additional tickers. This executor has not downloaded/hash-verified that native attachment.
- SEC eight-case report: parent-provided native Library ID `libfile_5669088d63f08191be64a80f093696db`, version 0, SHA-256 `46c33fdd4e786a4b5c8165405fda0e08998b7589c8bfa04e4e45a80e18afdd89`. Hash is parent-verified, not an independent executor download. Exact retained handoff SHA-256 `8df40e28370561d7b28a81ae1054f9e60b8e7bd5afda0eb24afe656d689fe34e`; original raw SEC/manual-news research ledgers were not recovered by that handoff.

## Scope and interpretation

The indexed families cover STRAT native cases and conformance, separate hybrid and fixed-comparator experiments, preserved visual/audit evidence, causal FTFC repair, 17-ticker earnings daily research, original ten two-hour paths and endpoint sidecar, twenty-ticker liquidity editions, original news sensitivity and 28-ticker coverage, SEC evidence handoff, acquisition/admission/refusal receipts, and database-route exports. Earlier editions, refusals and known limitations remain preserved. No holdout scoring or new acquisition was performed for this preservation.

SEC exploration covers APLD, DAL, BYRN, HELE, MAN, NVDA, TLRY and APOG. It is exploratory/as-of-tiered evidence, not validated forecasting. Later release facts cannot be treated as pre-release features; incidental outcome exposure remains disclosed. Official source citations are retained in the private report/handoff; a handoff is not the lost original research ledger.
